```
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,
   210→                "checks": checks,
   211→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→
   220→    if subj_avg is None:
   221→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   223→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   224→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   225→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   226→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   227→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   228→    else:
   229→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   230→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   231→        overall_score = round(
   232→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   233→            1,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   237→
   238→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   239→        pool_share = (
   240→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   241→        )
   242→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   243→        score = float(row["score"])
   244→        entries.append(
   245→            {
   246→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   247→                "pool": "mechanical",
   248→                "score": score,
   249→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   250→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   251→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   252→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   253→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   254→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   255→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   256→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   257→            }
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    for row in subjective_rows:
   261→        pool_share = (
   262→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   263→        )
   264→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   265→        score = float(row["score"])
   266→        entries.append(
   267→            {
   268→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   269→                "pool": "subjective",
   270→                "score": score,
   271→                "checks": 0.0,
   272→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   273→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   274→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   275→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   276→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   277→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   278→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    return {
   283→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   284→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   285→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   286→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   287→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   288→        "entries": entries,
   289→    }
   290→
   291→
   292→def compute_health_score(
   293→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   294→) -> float:
   295→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   296→    return float(
   297→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   298→            "overall_score"
   299→        ]
   300→    )
   301→
   302→
   303→def compute_score_bundle(
   304→    findings: dict,
   305→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:
   310→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   311→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   312→        findings,
   313→        potentials,
   314→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   315→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   320→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   321→
   322→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   323→        name: data
   324→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    return ScoreBundle(
   329→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   330→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ScoreBundle",
   407→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   408→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   409→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   410→    "compute_health_score",
   411→    "compute_score_bundle",
   412→    "compute_score_impact",
   413→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   414→]
   415→
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import re

    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(

    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (

    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0

    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )

   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }

   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""

   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
```

```
1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True
    81→    smell_id = fixer_name.replace("-", "_")
    82→    return any(
    83→        f.get("status") == "open"
    84→        and f.get("detector") == "smells"
    85→        and f.get("detail", {}).get("smell_id") == smell_id
    86→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
    87→    )
    88→
    89→
    90→def _append_auto_fix_actions(
    91→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    92→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    93→    supported: set[str] | None,
    94→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    95→    state: StateModel,
    96→) -> None:
    97→    """Append auto-fix/manual-fix actions for detectors with auto fixers."""
    98→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    99→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   100→            continue
   101→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   102→        if count == 0:
   103→            continue
   104→
   105→        impact = round(impact_for(detector, count), 1)
   106→        available_fixers = [
   107→            fixer
   108→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]
   109→            if (supported is None or fixer in supported)
   110→            and _fixer_has_applicable_findings(state, detector, fixer)
   111→        ]
   112→        if not available_fixers:
   113→            actions.append(
   114→                {
   115→                    "type": "manual_fix",
   116→                    "detector": detector,
   117→                    "count": count,
   118→                    "description": (
   119→                        f"{count} {detector} findings — inspect with "
   120→                        f"`desloppify next` and fix manually"
   121→                    ),
   122→                    "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   123→                    "impact": impact,
   124→                    "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   125→                }
   126→            )
   127→            continue
   128→
   129→        fixer = available_fixers[0]
   130→        actions.append(
   131→            {
   132→                "type": "auto_fix",
   133→                "detector": detector,
   134→                "count": count,
   135→                "description": (
   136→                    f"{count} {detector} findings — run "
   137→                    f"`desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run` to preview, then apply"
   138→                ),
   139→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   140→                "impact": impact,
   141→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   142→            }
   143→        )
   144→
   145→
   146→def _append_reorganize_actions(
   147→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   148→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   149→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   150→) -> None:
   151→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
   152→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   153→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
   154→            continue
   155→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   156→        if count == 0:
   157→            continue
   158→
   159→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
   160→        actions.append(
   161→            {
   162→                "type": "reorganize",
   163→                "detector": detector,
   164→                "count": count,
   165→                "description": f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}",
   166→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
   167→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   168→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→
   173→def _build_refactor_entry(
   174→    detector: str,
   175→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
   176→    count: int,
   177→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   178→) -> ActionItem:
   179→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
   180→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
   181→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
   182→
   183→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   184→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   185→        description = f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh subjective scores"
   186→    elif detector == "review":
   187→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   188→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
   189→        description = (
   190→            f"{count} review finding{suffix} need investigation — "
   191→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
   192→        )
   193→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
   194→    else:
   195→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
   196→        description = f"{count} {detector} findings — {guidance}"
   197→
   198→    return {
   199→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
   200→        "detector": detector,
   201→        "count": count,
   202→        "description": description,
   203→        "command": command,
   204→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
   205→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
   206→    }
   207→
   208→
   209→def _append_refactor_actions(
   210→    actions: list[ActionItem],
   211→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
   212→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
   213→) -> None:
   214→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
   215→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   216→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
   217→            continue
   218→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
   219→        if count == 0:
   220→            continue
   221→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
   222→
   223→
   224→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   225→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   226→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   227→    if gap <= 2.0:
   228→        return
   229→    actions.append(
   230→        {
   231→            "type": "debt_review",
   232→            "detector": None,
   233→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   234→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   235→            "gap": gap,
   236→        }
   237→    )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   241→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   242→    type_order = {
   243→        "issue_queue": 0,
   244→        "auto_fix": 1,
   245→        "reorganize": 2,
   246→        "refactor": 3,
   247→        "manual_fix": 4,
   248→        "debt_review": 5,
   249→    }
   250→    actions.sort(
   251→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   252→    )
   253→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   254→        action["priority"] = index
   255→    return actions
   256→
   257→
   258→def compute_actions(ctx: ActionContext) -> list[ActionItem]:
   259→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
   260→    actions: list[ActionItem] = []
   261→    impact_for = _impact_calculator(ctx.dimension_scores, ctx.state)
   262→    supported = supported_fixers(ctx.state, ctx.lang)
   263→
   264→    _append_auto_fix_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, supported, impact_for, ctx.state)
   265→    _append_reorganize_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   266→    _append_refactor_actions(actions, ctx.by_detector, impact_for)
   267→    _append_debt_action(actions, ctx.debt)
   268→
   269→    return _assign_priorities(actions)
   270→
```

```
1→"""Holistic review finding import workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    35→def parse_holistic_import_payload(
    36→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
    37→) -> tuple[list[ReviewFindingPayload], dict[str, Any] | None, list[str]]:
    38→    """Parse strict holistic import payload object."""
    39→    payload = parse_review_import_payload(data, mode_name="Holistic")
    40→    return payload.findings, payload.assessments, payload.reviewed_files

    43→def update_reviewed_file_cache(
    44→    state: dict[str, Any],
    45→    reviewed_files: list[str],
    46→    *,
    47→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
    48→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    49→) -> None:
    50→    """Refresh per-file review cache entries from holistic payload metadata."""
    51→    refresh_review_file_cache(
    52→        state,
    53→        reviewed_files=reviewed_files,
    54→        findings_by_file=None,
    55→        project_root=project_root,
    56→        hash_file_fn=hash_file,
    57→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→_POSITIVE_PREFIXES = (
    62→    "good ",
    63→    "well ",
    64→    "strong ",
    65→    "clean ",
    66→    "excellent ",
    67→    "nice ",
    68→    "solid ",
    69→)
    70→
    71→
    72→def _validate_and_build_findings(
    73→    findings_list: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
    74→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    75→    lang_name: str,
    76→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    77→    """Validate raw holistic findings and build state-ready finding dicts.
    78→
    79→    Returns (review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns).
    80→    """
    81→    review_findings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    82→    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    83→    dismissed_concerns: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    84→    allowed_dimensions = {
    85→        dim for dim in holistic_prompts if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    for idx, raw_finding in enumerate(findings_list):
    89→        finding, finding_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
    90→            raw_finding,
    91→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
    92→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions,
    93→            allow_dismissed=True,
    94→        )
    95→        if finding_errors:
    96→            skipped.append(
    97→                {
    98→                    "index": idx,
    99→                    "missing": finding_errors,
   100→                    "identifier": (
   101→                        raw_finding.get("identifier", "<none>")
   102→                        if isinstance(raw_finding, dict)
   103→                        else "<none>"
   104→                    ),
   105→                }
   106→            )
   107→            continue
   108→        assert finding is not None
   109→
   110→        # Handle dismissed concern verdicts (no dimension/summary required).
   111→        if finding.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed":
   112→            fp = finding.get("concern_fingerprint", "")
   113→            if fp:
   114→                dismissed_concerns.append(
   115→                    {
   116→                        "fingerprint": fp,
   117→                        "concern_type": finding.get("concern_type", ""),
   118→                        "concern_file": finding.get("concern_file", ""),
   119→                        "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   120→                    }
   121→                )
   122→            continue
   123→
   124→        # Safety net: skip positive observations that slipped past the prompt.
   125→        summary_text = str(finding.get("summary", ""))
   126→        if summary_text.lower().startswith(_POSITIVE_PREFIXES):
   127→            skipped.append(
   128→                {
   129→                    "index": idx,
   130→                    "missing": ["positive observation (not a defect)"],
   131→                    "identifier": finding.get("identifier", "<none>"),
   132→                }
   133→            )
   134→            continue
   135→
   136→        dimension = finding["dimension"]
   137→
   138→        # Confirmed concern verdicts become "concerns" detector findings.
   139→        is_confirmed_concern = finding.get("concern_verdict") == "confirmed"
   140→        detector = "concerns" if is_confirmed_concern else "review"
   141→
   142→        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(summary_text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
   143→        detail: dict[str, Any] = {
   144→            "holistic": True,
   145→            "dimension": dimension,
   146→            "related_files": finding["related_files"],
   147→            "evidence": finding["evidence"],
   148→            "suggestion": finding.get("suggestion", ""),
   149→            "reasoning": finding.get("reasoning", ""),
   150→        }
   151→        if is_confirmed_concern:
   152→            detail["concern_type"] = finding.get("concern_type", "")
   153→            detail["concern_verdict"] = "confirmed"
   154→
   155→        prefix = "concern" if is_confirmed_concern else "holistic"
   156→        file = finding.get("concern_file", "") if is_confirmed_concern else ""
   157→        confidence = normalize_review_confidence(finding.get("confidence", "low"))
   158→        imported = make_finding(
   159→            detector=detector,
   160→            file=file,
   161→            name=f"{prefix}::{dimension}::{finding['identifier']}::{content_hash}",
   162→            tier=review_tier(confidence, holistic=True),
   163→            confidence=confidence,
   164→            summary=summary_text,
   165→            detail=detail,
   166→        )
   167→        imported["lang"] = lang_name
   168→        review_findings.append(imported)
   169→
   170→    return review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns
   171→
   172→
   173→def _collect_imported_dimensions(
   174→    *,
   175→    findings_list: list[ReviewFindingPayload],
   176→    review_findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   177→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None,
   178→    review_scope: ReviewScopePayload | dict[str, Any] | None,
   179→    valid_dimensions: set[str],
   180→) -> set[str]:
   181→    """Return normalized dimensions this import explicitly covered."""
   182→    imported_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   183→
   184→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   185→        scope_dims = review_scope.get("imported_dimensions")
   186→        if isinstance(scope_dims, list):
   187→            for raw_dim in scope_dims:
   188→                normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   189→                if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   190→                    imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   191→
   192→    for finding in findings_list:
   193→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   194→            continue
   195→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(finding.get("dimension", "")))
   196→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   197→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   198→
   199→    for finding in review_findings:
   200→        detail = finding.get("detail")
   201→        if not isinstance(detail, dict):
   202→            continue
   203→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   204→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   205→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   206→
   207→    for raw_dim in (assessments or {}):
   208→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_dim))
   209→        if normalized in valid_dimensions:
   210→            imported_dimensions.add(normalized)
   211→
   212→    return imported_dimensions
   213→
   214→
   215→def _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   216→    state: dict[str, Any],
   217→    new_ids: set[str],
   218→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   219→    utc_now_fn,
   220→    *,
   221→    imported_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   222→    full_sweep_included: bool | None = None,
   223→) -> None:
   224→    """Auto-resolve open holistic findings not present in the latest import."""
   225→    scope_dimensions = {
   226→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   227→        for dim in (imported_dimensions or set())
   228→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   229→    }
   230→    scoped_reimport = full_sweep_included is False
   231→    # Partial re-import with unknown dimension scope: do not auto-resolve.
   232→    if scoped_reimport and not scope_dimensions:
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    def _should_resolve(finding: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   236→        if finding.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns"):
   237→            return False
   238→        detail = finding.get("detail")
   239→        if not isinstance(detail, dict) or not detail.get("holistic"):
   240→            return False
   241→        if not scoped_reimport:
   242→            return True
   243→        dimension = normalize_dimension_name(str(detail.get("dimension", "")))
   244→        return dimension in scope_dimensions
   245→
   246→    auto_resolve_review_findings(
   247→        state,
   248→        new_ids=new_ids,
   249→        diff=diff,
   250→        note="not reported in latest holistic re-import",
   251→        should_resolve=_should_resolve,
   252→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   253→    )
   254→
   255→
   256→def import_holistic_findings(
   257→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   258→    state: dict[str, Any],
   259→    lang_name: str,
   260→    *,
   261→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
   262→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   263→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   264→    """Import holistic (codebase-wide) findings into state."""
   265→    payload: ReviewImportEnvelope = parse_review_import_payload(
   266→        findings_data,
   267→        mode_name="Holistic",
   268→    )
   269→    findings_list = payload.findings
   270→    assessments = payload.assessments
   271→    reviewed_files = payload.reviewed_files
   272→    review_scope = findings_data.get("review_scope", {})
   273→    if not isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   274→        review_scope = {}
   275→    scope_full_sweep = review_scope.get("full_sweep_included")
   276→    if not isinstance(scope_full_sweep, bool):
   277→        scope_full_sweep = None
   278→    if assessments:
   279→        store_assessments(
   280→            state,
   281→            assessments,
   282→            source="holistic",
   283→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   284→        )
   285→
   286→    _, holistic_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   287→    valid_dimensions = {
   288→        normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   289→        for dim in holistic_prompts
   290→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   291→    }
   292→    review_findings, skipped, dismissed_concerns = _validate_and_build_findings(
   293→        findings_list, holistic_prompts, lang_name
   294→    )
   295→    imported_dimensions = _collect_imported_dimensions(
   296→        findings_list=findings_list,
   297→        review_findings=review_findings,
   298→        assessments=assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else None,
   299→        review_scope=review_scope,
   300→        valid_dimensions=valid_dimensions,
   301→    )
   302→
   303→    # Store dismissed concern verdicts for suppression in future concern generation.
   304→    if dismissed_concerns:
   305→        from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
   306→
   307→        store = state.setdefault("concern_dismissals", {})
   308→        now = utc_now_fn()
   309→        # Compute current concerns to get source_finding_ids for each fingerprint.
   310→        current_concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   311→        concern_sources = {
   312→            c.fingerprint: list(c.source_findings) for c in current_concerns
   313→        }
   314→        for dc in dismissed_concerns:
   315→            fp = dc["fingerprint"]
   316→            store[fp] = {
   317→                "dismissed_at": now,
   318→                "reasoning": dc.get("reasoning", ""),
   319→                "concern_type": dc.get("concern_type", ""),
   320→                "concern_file": dc.get("concern_file", ""),
   321→                "source_finding_ids": concern_sources.get(fp, []),
   322→            }
   323→
   324→    potentials = _lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   325→    existing_review = potentials.get("review", 0)
   326→    potentials["review"] = max(existing_review, HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL)
   327→
   328→    concern_count = sum(1 for f in review_findings if f.get("detector") == "concerns")
   329→    if concern_count:
   330→        potentials["concerns"] = max(potentials.get("concerns", 0), concern_count)
   331→
   332→    merge_potentials_dict: dict[str, int] = {"review": potentials.get("review", 0)}
   333→    if potentials.get("concerns", 0) > 0:
   334→        merge_potentials_dict["concerns"] = potentials["concerns"]
   335→
   336→    diff = merge_scan(
   337→        state,
   338→        review_findings,
   339→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   340→            lang=lang_name,
   341→            potentials=merge_potentials_dict,
   342→            merge_potentials=True,
   343→        ),
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    new_ids = {finding["id"] for finding in review_findings}
   347→    _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   348→        state,
   349→        new_ids,
   350→        diff,
   351→        utc_now_fn,
   352→        imported_dimensions=imported_dimensions,
   353→        full_sweep_included=scope_full_sweep,
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    if skipped:
   357→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
   358→        diff["skipped_details"] = skipped
   359→
   360→    update_reviewed_file_cache(
   361→        state,
   362→        reviewed_files,
   363→        project_root=project_root,
   364→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   365→    )
   366→    resolve_reviewed_file_coverage_findings(
   367→        state,
   368→        diff,
   369→        reviewed_files,
   370→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   371→    )
   372→    update_holistic_review_cache(
   373→        state,
   374→        findings_list,
   375→        lang_name=lang_name,
   376→        review_scope=review_scope,
   377→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   378→    )
   379→    resolve_holistic_coverage_findings(state, diff, utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn)
   380→
   381→    # Clean up dismissals whose source findings were all resolved — runs after
   382→    # all finding mutations (merge_scan, auto_resolve, coverage resolve) so it
   383→    # sees the final state.
   384→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import cleanup_stale_dismissals
   385→
   386→    cleanup_stale_dismissals(state)
   387→
   388→    return diff
   389→
   390→
   391→def _resolve_total_files(state: dict[str, Any], lang_name: str | None) -> int:
   392→    """Best-effort total file count from codebase_metrics or review cache."""
   393→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   394→    fallback = len(review_cache.get("files", {}))
   395→
   396→    codebase_metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
   397→    if not isinstance(codebase_metrics, dict):
   398→        return fallback
   399→
   400→    # Try language-specific metrics first, then global.
   401→    sources = []
   402→    if lang_name:
   403→        lang_metrics = codebase_metrics.get(lang_name)
   404→        if isinstance(lang_metrics, dict):
   405→            sources.append(lang_metrics)
   406→    sources.append(codebase_metrics)
   407→
   408→    for source in sources:
   409→        metric_total = source.get("total_files")
   410→        if isinstance(metric_total, int) and metric_total > 0:
   411→            return metric_total
   412→
   413→    return fallback
   414→
   415→
   416→def update_holistic_review_cache(
   417→    state: dict[str, Any],
   418→    findings_data: list[dict],
   419→    *,
   420→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   421→    review_scope: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   422→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   423→) -> None:
   424→    """Store holistic review metadata in review_cache."""
   425→    review_cache = state.setdefault("review_cache", {})
   426→    now = utc_now_fn()
   427→    _, holistic_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name or "")
   428→
   429→    valid = [
   430→        finding
   431→        for finding in findings_data
   432→        if all(
   433→            key in finding
   434→            for key in ("dimension", "identifier", "summary", "confidence")
   435→        )
   436→        and finding["dimension"] in holistic_prompts
   437→    ]
   438→
   439→    resolved_total_files: int
   440→    total_override = (
   441→        review_scope.get("total_files")
   442→        if isinstance(review_scope, dict)
   443→        else None
   444→    )
   445→    if (
   446→        isinstance(total_override, int)
   447→        and not isinstance(total_override, bool)
   448→        and total_override > 0
   449→    ):
   450→        resolved_total_files = total_override
   451→    else:
   452→        resolved_total_files = _resolve_total_files(state, lang_name)
   453→
   454→    holistic_entry: dict[str, Any] = {
   455→        "reviewed_at": now,
   456→        "file_count_at_review": resolved_total_files,
   457→        "finding_count": len(valid),
   458→    }
   459→    if isinstance(review_scope, dict):
   460→        reviewed_files_count = review_scope.get("reviewed_files_count")
   461→        if (
   462→            isinstance(reviewed_files_count, int)
   463→            and not isinstance(reviewed_files_count, bool)
   464→            and reviewed_files_count >= 0
   465→        ):
   466→            holistic_entry["reviewed_files_count"] = reviewed_files_count
   467→        full_sweep_included = review_scope.get("full_sweep_included")
   468→        if isinstance(full_sweep_included, bool):
   469→            holistic_entry["full_sweep_included"] = full_sweep_included
   470→
   471→    review_cache["holistic"] = holistic_entry
   472→
   473→
   474→def resolve_holistic_coverage_findings(
   475→    state: dict[str, Any],
   476→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   477→    *,
   478→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   479→) -> None:
   480→    """Resolve stale holistic coverage entries after successful holistic import."""
   481→    now = utc_now_fn()
   482→    for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values():
   483→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   484→            continue
   485→        if finding.get("detector") != "subjective_review":
   486→            continue
   487→
   488→        finding_id = finding.get("id", "")
   489→        if (
   490→            "::holistic_unreviewed" not in finding_id
   491→            and "::holistic_stale" not in finding_id
   492→        ):
   493→            continue
   494→
   495→        finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   496→        finding["resolved_at"] = now
   497→        finding["note"] = "resolved by holistic review import"
   498→        finding["resolution_attestation"] = {
   499→            "kind": "agent_import",
   500→            "text": "Holistic review refreshed; coverage marker superseded",
   501→            "attested_at": now,
   502→            "scan_verified": False,
   503→        }
   504→        diff["auto_resolved"] += 1
   505→
   506→
   507→def resolve_reviewed_file_coverage_findings(
   508→    state: dict[str, Any],
   509→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   510→    reviewed_files: list[str],
   511→    *,
   512→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   513→) -> None:
   514→    """Resolve per-file subjective coverage markers for freshly reviewed files."""
   515→    if not reviewed_files:
   516→        return
   517→
   518→    reviewed_set = {path for path in reviewed_files if isinstance(path, str) and path}
   519→    if not reviewed_set:
   520→        return
   521→
   522→    now = utc_now_fn()
   523→    for finding in state.get("findings", {}).values():
   524→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   525→            continue
   526→        if finding.get("detector") != "subjective_review":
   527→            continue
   528→
   529→        finding_id = finding.get("id", "")
   530→        if "::holistic_unreviewed" in finding_id or "::holistic_stale" in finding_id:
   531→            continue
   532→
   533→        finding_file = finding.get("file", "")
   534→        if finding_file not in reviewed_set:
   535→            continue
   536→
   537→        finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   538→        finding["resolved_at"] = now
   539→        finding["note"] = "resolved by reviewed_files cache refresh"
   540→        finding["resolution_attestation"] = {
   541→            "kind": "agent_import",
   542→            "text": "Per-file review cache refreshed for this file",
   543→            "attested_at": now,
   544→            "scan_verified": False,
   545→        }
   546→        diff["auto_resolved"] += 1
   547→
```

```
1→"""Dimension analysis and debt computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
     6→from desloppify.scoring import (
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    TIER_WEIGHTS,
     9→    compute_score_impact,
    10→    merge_potentials,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.state import StateModel, path_scoped_findings
    13→
    14→
    15→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    16→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    17→    if not dim_scores:
    18→        return {}
    19→
    20→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    21→    return {
    22→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    23→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    24→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    29→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    30→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    31→        (
    32→            (name, ds)
    33→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    34→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    35→        ),
    36→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    37→    )
    38→
    39→    lowest = []
    40→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    41→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    42→        issues = ds["issues"]
    43→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    44→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    45→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    46→            potentials=potentials,
    47→        )
    48→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    49→        entry = {
    50→            "name": name,
    51→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    52→            "issues": issues,
    53→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    54→        }
    55→        if is_subjective:
    56→            entry["subjective"] = True
    57→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    58→        lowest.append(entry)
    59→    return lowest
    60→
    61→
    62→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    63→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    64→    biggest_gap = []
    65→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(
    66→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    67→    )
    68→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    69→        lenient = ds["score"]
    70→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    71→        gap = lenient - strict
    72→        if gap > 1.0:
    73→            wontfix_count = sum(
    74→                1
    75→                for f in scoped.values()
    76→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    77→                and _finding_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    78→            )
    79→            biggest_gap.append(
    80→                {
    81→                    "name": name,
    82→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    83→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    84→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    85→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    86→                }
    87→            )
    88→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    89→    return biggest_gap
    90→
    91→
    92→def _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    93→    """Build summary entries for dimensions that have not moved recently."""
    94→    if len(history) < 3:
    95→        return []
    96→
    97→    stagnant = []
    98→    for name in dim_scores:
    99→        scores = _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name, history)
   100→        if len(scores) < 3 or max(scores) - min(scores) > 0.5:
   101→            continue
   102→        stagnant.append(
   103→            {
   104→                "name": name,
   105→                "strict": round(dim_scores[name].get("strict", dim_scores[name]["score"]), 1),
   106→                "stuck_scans": len(scores),
   107→            }
   108→        )
   109→    return stagnant
   110→
   111→
   112→def _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name: str, history: list[dict]) -> list[float]:
   113→    """Collect recent strict score samples for one dimension."""
   114→    scores: list[float] = []
   115→    for history_entry in history[-5:]:
   116→        dim_entry = (history_entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(name)
   117→        if not dim_entry:
   118→            continue
   119→        strict_value = dim_entry.get("strict", dim_entry.get("score"))
   120→        if strict_value is not None:
   121→            scores.append(float(strict_value))
   122→    return scores
   123→
   124→
   125→def _dominant_detector_impact(
   126→    *,
   127→    dim_scores: dict,
   128→    detectors: dict,
   129→    potentials: dict,
   130→) -> float:
   131→    """Estimate impact using the most consequential detector in a dimension."""
   132→    normalized_scores = {
   133→        key: {
   134→            "score": value["score"],
   135→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   136→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   137→        }
   138→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   139→    }
   140→    impact = 0.0
   141→    for detector_name, detector_data in detectors.items():
   142→        issue_count = int(detector_data.get("issues", 0) or 0)
   143→        if issue_count <= 0:
   144→            continue
   145→        detector_impact = compute_score_impact(
   146→            normalized_scores,
   147→            potentials,
   148→            detector_name,
   149→            issue_count,
   150→        )
   151→        impact = max(impact, detector_impact)
   152→    return impact
   153→
   154→
   155→def _finding_in_dimension(finding: dict, dim_name: str, dim_scores: dict) -> bool:
   156→    """Check if a finding's detector belongs to a dimension."""
   157→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   158→    if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   159→        detector = "structural"
   160→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   161→        if dim.name == dim_name and detector in dim.detectors:
   162→            return True
   163→    return False
   164→
   165→
   166→def _analyze_debt(dim_scores: dict, findings: dict, history: list[dict]) -> dict:
   167→    """Compute wontfix debt analysis."""
   168→    # Count wontfix
   169→    wontfix_count = sum(1 for f in findings.values() if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   170→
   171→    # Compute gap per dimension
   172→    worst_dim = None
   173→    worst_gap = 0.0
   174→    overall_lenient = 0.0
   175→    overall_strict = 0.0
   176→    if dim_scores:
   177→        w_sum_l = 0.0
   178→        w_sum_s = 0.0
   179→        w_total = 0.0
   180→        for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
   181→            tier = ds.get("tier", 3)
   182→            w = TIER_WEIGHTS.get(tier, 2)
   183→            w_sum_l += ds["score"] * w
   184→            w_sum_s += ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) * w
   185→            w_total += w
   186→            gap = ds["score"] - ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   187→            if gap > worst_gap:
   188→                worst_gap = gap
   189→                worst_dim = name
   190→        if w_total > 0:
   191→            overall_lenient = round(w_sum_l / w_total, 1)
   192→            overall_strict = round(w_sum_s / w_total, 1)
   193→
   194→    overall_gap = round(overall_lenient - overall_strict, 1)
   195→
   196→    # Trend from history
   197→    trend = "stable"
   198→    if len(history) >= 3:
   199→        gaps = []
   200→        for h in history[-5:]:
   201→            hs = h.get("strict_score")
   202→            hl = h.get("overall_score")
   203→            if hs is not None and hl is not None:
   204→                gaps.append(hl - hs)
   205→        if len(gaps) >= 2:
   206→            if gaps[-1] > gaps[0] + 0.5:
   207→                trend = "growing"
   208→            elif gaps[-1] < gaps[0] - 0.5:
   209→                trend = "shrinking"
   210→
   211→    return {
   212→        "overall_gap": overall_gap,
   213→        "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
   214→        "worst_dimension": worst_dim,
   215→        "worst_gap": round(worst_gap, 1),
   216→        "trend": trend,
   217→    }
   218→
```

```
1→"""Shared parsing/assessment helpers for review finding imports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import Any
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions import normalize_dimension_name
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts import (
    14→    ReviewFindingPayload,
    15→    ReviewImportPayload,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class ReviewImportEnvelope:
    22→    """Validated shared payload shape for review imports."""
    23→
    24→    findings: list[ReviewFindingPayload]
    25→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None
    26→    reviewed_files: list[str]
    27→
    28→
    29→def _review_file_cache(state: dict[str, Any]) -> dict:
    30→    """Access ``state["review_cache"]["files"]``, creating if absent."""
    31→    return state.setdefault("review_cache", {}).setdefault("files", {})
    32→
    33→
    34→def _lang_potentials(state: dict[str, Any], lang_name: str) -> dict:
    35→    """Access ``state["potentials"][lang_name]``, creating if absent."""
    36→    return state.setdefault("potentials", {}).setdefault(lang_name, {})
    37→
    38→
    39→def store_assessments(
    40→    state: dict[str, Any],
    41→    assessments: dict[str, Any],
    42→    source: str,
    43→    *,
    44→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    45→) -> None:
    46→    """Store dimension assessments in state.
    47→
    48→    *assessments*: ``{dim_name: score}`` or ``{dim_name: {score, ...}}``.
    49→    *source*: ``"per_file"`` or ``"holistic"``.
    50→
    51→    Holistic assessments overwrite per-file for the same dimension.
    52→    Per-file assessments don't overwrite holistic.
    53→    """
    54→    store = state.setdefault("subjective_assessments", {})
    55→    now = utc_now_fn()
    56→
    57→    for dimension_name, value in assessments.items():
    58→        value_obj = value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}
    59→        score = value if isinstance(value, int | float) else value_obj.get("score", 0)
    60→        score = max(0, min(100, score))
    61→        [REDACTED](str(dimension_name))
    62→        if not dimension_key:
    63→            continue
    64→
    65→        existing = store.get(dimension_key)
    66→        if existing and existing.get("source") == "holistic" and source == "per_file":
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        cleaned_components: list[str] = []
    70→        components = value_obj.get("components")
    71→        if isinstance(components, list):
    72→            cleaned_components = [
    73→                str(item).strip()
    74→                for item in components
    75→                if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
    76→            ]
    77→
    78→        component_scores = value_obj.get("component_scores")
    79→        cleaned_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    80→        if isinstance(component_scores, dict):
    81→            for key, raw in component_scores.items():
    82→                if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
    83→                    continue
    84→                if not is_numeric(raw):
    85→                    continue
    86→                cleaned_scores[key.strip()] = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    87→
    88→        store[dimension_key] = {
    89→            "score": score,
    90→            "source": source,
    91→            "assessed_at": now,
    92→            **({"components": cleaned_components} if cleaned_components else {}),
    93→            **({"component_scores": cleaned_scores} if cleaned_scores else {}),
    94→        }
    95→
    96→
    97→def extract_reviewed_files(data: list[dict] | dict) -> list[str]:
    98→    """Parse optional reviewed-file list from import payload."""
    99→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   100→        return []
   101→    raw = data.get("reviewed_files")
   102→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   103→        return []
   104→
   105→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   106→    seen: set[str] = set()
   107→    for item in raw:
   108→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   109→            continue
   110→        path = item.strip()
   111→        if not path or path in seen:
   112→            continue
   113→        seen.add(path)
   114→        reviewed.append(path)
   115→    return reviewed
   116→
   117→
   118→def parse_review_import_payload(
   119→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
   120→    *,
   121→    mode_name: str,
   122→) -> ReviewImportEnvelope:
   123→    """Parse shared review import payload shape for per-file/holistic flows."""
   124→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   125→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload must be a JSON object")
   126→
   127→    findings = data.get("findings", [])
   128→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   129→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings' must be a list")
   130→    for idx, finding in enumerate(findings):
   131→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   132→            raise ValueError(
   133→                f"{mode_name} review import payload 'findings[{idx}]' must be an object"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→    assessments = data.get("assessments")
   137→    if assessments is not None and not isinstance(assessments, dict):
   138→        raise ValueError(
   139→            f"{mode_name} review import payload 'assessments' must be an object"
   140→        )
   141→    return ReviewImportEnvelope(
   142→        findings=findings,
   143→        assessments=assessments,
   144→        reviewed_files=extract_reviewed_files(data),
   145→    )
   146→
   147→
   148→def normalize_review_confidence(value: object) -> str:
   149→    """Normalize review confidence labels to high/medium/low."""
   150→    confidence = str(value).strip().lower()
   151→    return confidence if confidence in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "low"
   152→
   153→
   154→def review_tier(confidence: str, *, holistic: bool) -> int:
   155→    """Derive natural tier from review confidence and scope."""
   156→    if confidence == "high":
   157→        return 1 if holistic else 2
   158→    if confidence == "medium":
   159→        return 2 if holistic else 3
   160→    return 3
   161→
   162→
   163→def resolve_import_project_root(project_root: Path | str | None) -> Path:
   164→    """Resolve optional import project root to an absolute path."""
   165→    if project_root is None:
   166→        return get_project_root()
   167→    return Path(project_root).resolve()
   168→
   169→
   170→def upsert_review_cache_entry(
   171→    file_cache: dict[str, Any],
   172→    file_path: str,
   173→    *,
   174→    project_root: Path,
   175→    hash_file_fn,
   176→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   177→    finding_count: int | None = None,
   178→) -> None:
   179→    """Write one normalized review-cache entry for a reviewed file."""
   180→    absolute = project_root / file_path
   181→    content_hash = hash_file_fn(str(absolute)) if absolute.exists() else ""
   182→    if finding_count is None:
   183→        previous = file_cache.get(file_path, {})
   184→        count = previous.get("finding_count", 0) if isinstance(previous, dict) else 0
   185→        finding_count = count if isinstance(count, int) else 0
   186→    file_cache[file_path] = {
   187→        "content_hash": content_hash,
   188→        "reviewed_at": utc_now_fn(),
   189→        "finding_count": max(0, int(finding_count)),
   190→    }
   191→
   192→
   193→def refresh_review_file_cache(
   194→    state: dict[str, Any],
   195→    *,
   196→    reviewed_files: list[str] | None,
   197→    findings_by_file: dict[str, int | None] | None = None,
   198→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
   199→    hash_file_fn,
   200→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   201→) -> None:
   202→    """Refresh normalized review cache entries for all reviewed files."""
   203→    file_cache = _review_file_cache(state)
   204→    resolved_project_root = resolve_import_project_root(project_root)
   205→    counts = findings_by_file or {}
   206→
   207→    reviewed_set = set(counts)
   208→    if reviewed_files:
   209→        reviewed_set.update(
   210→            str(file_path).strip()
   211→            for file_path in reviewed_files
   212→            if isinstance(file_path, str) and str(file_path).strip()
   213→        )
   214→
   215→    for file_path in reviewed_set:
   216→        upsert_review_cache_entry(
   217→            file_cache,
   218→            file_path,
   219→            project_root=resolved_project_root,
   220→            hash_file_fn=hash_file_fn,
   221→            utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
   222→            finding_count=counts.get(file_path),
   223→        )
   224→
   225→
   226→def auto_resolve_review_findings(
   227→    state: dict[str, Any],
   228→    *,
   229→    new_ids: set[str],
   230→    diff: dict[str, Any],
   231→    note: str,
   232→    should_resolve: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], bool],
   233→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   234→) -> None:
   235→    """Auto-resolve stale open review findings that match a scope predicate."""
   236→    diff.setdefault("auto_resolved", 0)
   237→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
   238→        if finding_id in new_ids or finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if not should_resolve(finding):
   241→            continue
   242→        finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   243→        finding["resolved_at"] = utc_now_fn()
   244→        finding["note"] = note
   245→        diff["auto_resolved"] += 1
   246→
```

```
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass

     9→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap

    16→from desloppify.state import Finding

    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
```

```
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(

    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low

    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
```
