```
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→
    76→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    80→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    81→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    82→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    83→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    84→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    85→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    86→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    87→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    88→
    89→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    90→
    91→    queue = build_work_queue(
    92→        state,
    93→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    94→            tier=tier,
    95→            count=count,
    96→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    97→            scope=scope,
    98→            status=status,
    99→            include_subjective=True,
   100→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   101→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   102→            explain=explain,
   103→        ),
   104→    )
   105→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   106→
   107→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   108→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   109→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   110→        state,
   111→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   115→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative
   116→    )
   117→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   118→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   119→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   120→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   121→        state,
   122→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   123→    )
   124→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   125→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   126→    ]
   127→    write_query(payload)
   128→
   129→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   130→    if output_file:
   131→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   132→            output_file,
   133→            payload,
   134→            len(items),
   135→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   136→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   137→        ):
   138→            return
   139→        raise SystemExit(1)
   140→
   141→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   145→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   147→        return
   148→
   149→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   150→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   151→        state.get("findings", {}),
   152→        state.get("scan_path"),
   153→    )
   154→    potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   155→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   156→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   157→        potentials=potentials or None,
   158→    )
   159→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   160→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   161→        state,
   162→        dim_scores,
   163→        findings_scoped,
   164→        strict_score=strict_score,
   165→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   166→    )
   167→    print()
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   171→
```

```
    34→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    35→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    36→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    37→
    38→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    39→    if not concerns:
    40→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    44→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    45→
    46→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    51→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    54→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    55→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    56→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    57→            if reasoning:
    58→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    59→        print()
    60→
    61→
    62→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    63→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    64→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    65→    state = runtime.state
    66→    config = runtime.config
    67→
    68→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    72→    if stale_warning:
    73→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    74→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    75→    if skill_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    # Handle "show concerns" as a special view.
    79→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
    80→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    81→        lang = resolve_lang(args)
    82→        _show_concerns(state, lang.name if lang else None)
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
    86→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    87→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
    88→    if not ok or pattern is None:
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    matches = load_matches(state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic)
    92→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    93→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    94→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    95→        state,
    96→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
    97→    )
    98→
    99→    if not matches:
   100→        # Try interpreting the pattern as a dimension name/key
   101→        dim_matches, dim_display = load_matches_for_dimension(
   102→            state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter
   103→        )
   104→        if dim_matches:
   105→            matches = dim_matches
   106→            pattern = dim_display or pattern
   107→        else:
   108→            # Check if this is a subjective-only dimension
   109→            lowered = pattern_raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_") if pattern_raw else ""
   110→            _matched_subjective_dim = False
   111→            if lowered:
   112→                dim_lookup = {dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_"): dim for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS}
   113→                matched_dim = dim_lookup.get(lowered)
   114→                if matched_dim and hasattr(matched_dim, "detectors"):
   115→                    subjective_detectors = {"subjective_assessment", "subjective_review"}
   116→                    if subjective_detectors & set(matched_dim.detectors):
   117→                        _matched_subjective_dim = True
   118→                        print(colorize(
   119→                            f"  '{pattern_raw.strip()}' is a subjective dimension — its score comes from design reviews, not code findings.",
   120→                            "yellow",
   121→                        ))
   122→                        print(colorize(
   123→                            "  Run `desloppify show subjective` to see all subjective dimension scores.",
   124→                            "dim",
   125→                        ))
   126→                        show_subjective_followup(
   127→                            state,
   128→                            target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   129→                        )
   130→                        return
   131→
   132→            # "show subjective" with no findings still shows the subjective dashboard
   133→            is_subjective_view = pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() in (
   134→                "subjective", "subjective_review",
   135→            )
   136→            if is_subjective_view:
   137→                print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))
   138→                write_query(
   139→                    {
   140→                        "command": "show",
   141→                        "query": pattern,
   142→                        "status_filter": status_filter,
   143→                        "total": 0,
   144→                        "findings": [],
   145→                        "narrative": narrative,
   146→                    }
   147→                )
   148→                show_subjective_followup(
   149→                    state,
   150→                    target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   151→                )
   152→                return
   153→
   154→            hint = _detector_names_hint()
   155→            print(
   156→                colorize(
   157→                    f"No {status_filter} findings matching: {pattern}",
   158→                    "yellow",
   159→                )
   160→            )
   161→            print(
   162→                colorize(
   163→                    f"  Try: show <detector>, show <file>, or show subjective. "
   164→                    f"Detectors: {hint}",
   165→                    "dim",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→            write_query(

   171→                    "query": pattern,

   173→                    "total": 0,
   174→                    "findings": [],
   175→                    "narrative": narrative,
   176→                }
   177→            )
   178→            return
   179→
   180→    (
   181→        surfaced_matches,
   182→        hidden_by_detector,
   183→        noise_budget,
   184→        global_noise_budget,
   185→        budget_warning,
   186→    ) = resolve_noise(
   187→        config,
   188→        matches,
   189→    )
   190→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   191→
   192→    payload = build_show_payload(
   193→        surfaced_matches,
   194→        pattern,
   195→        status_filter,
   196→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   197→            total_matches=len(matches),
   198→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   199→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   200→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   201→        ),
   202→    )
   203→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   204→
   205→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   206→    if output_file:
   207→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   208→            return
   209→        raise SystemExit(1)
   210→
   211→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   212→    render_findings(
   213→        surfaced_matches,
   214→        pattern=pattern,
   215→        status_filter=status_filter,
   216→        show_code=show_code,
   217→        top=top,
   218→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   219→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   220→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   221→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   222→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   223→    )
   224→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches)
   225→    show_subjective_followup(
   226→        state,
   227→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   228→    )
   229→
   230→    # Phase 5: naming guide for subjective views
   231→    if pattern_raw and pattern_raw.strip().lower() in ("subjective", "subjective_review"):
   232→        print(colorize("  Related views:", "dim"))
   233→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   234→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
```

```
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():

    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:

    81→

    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:

   207→
   208→
   209→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   210→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   211→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   212→        return
   213→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   214→    if not explain:
   215→        return
   216→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   217→    if available:
   218→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   219→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   220→
   221→
   222→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   223→    if queue.get("items"):
   224→        return False
   225→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   226→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   227→    if tier is not None:
   228→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   229→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   230→        if available:
   231→            commands = " | ".join(
   232→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   233→            )
   234→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   235→    return True
   236→
   237→
   238→def render_terminal_items(
   239→    items: list[dict],
   240→    dim_scores: dict,
   241→    findings_scoped: dict,
   242→    *,
   243→    group: str,
   244→    explain: bool,
   245→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   246→) -> None:
   247→    if group != "item":
   248→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   249→        return
   250→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   251→        if idx > 0:
   252→            print()
   253→        label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]" if len(items) > 1 else "  Next item"
   254→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   255→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   256→
   257→
   258→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   259→    if len(items) != 1 or items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   260→        return
   261→    item = items[0]
   262→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   263→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   264→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   265→        primary = item.get(
   266→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   267→        )
   268→        print(f"    {primary}")
   269→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   270→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   274→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   275→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   276→        print(f"    {primary}")
   277→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   278→    else:
   279→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   280→
   281→    print(
   282→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   283→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   284→    )
   285→    print(
   286→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   287→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   288→    )
   289→
   290→
   291→def render_followup_nudges(
   292→    state: dict,
   293→    dim_scores: dict,
   294→    findings_scoped: dict,
   295→    *,
   296→    strict_score: float | None,
   297→    target_strict_score: float,
   298→) -> None:
   299→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   300→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   301→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   302→        state,
   303→        subjective_entries,
   304→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   305→        max_quality_items=3,
   306→        max_integrity_items=5,
   307→    )
   308→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   309→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   310→    if strict_score is not None:
   311→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   312→        if gap > 0:
   313→            print(
   314→                colorize(
   315→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   316→                    "cyan",
   317→                )
   318→            )
   319→        else:
   320→            print(
   321→                colorize(
   322→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   323→                    "green",
   324→                )
   325→            )
   326→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   327→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   328→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   329→
   330→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   331→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   332→        findings_scoped
   333→    )
   334→    parts: list[str] = []
   335→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   336→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   337→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   338→    open_review = [
   339→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   340→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   341→    ]
   342→    if low_dims:
   343→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   344→    if stale_count:
   345→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   346→    if unassessed_count:
   347→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   348→    if len(open_review):
   349→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   350→    if coverage_open > 0:
   351→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   352→
   353→    if parts:
   354→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   355→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→__all__ = [
   359→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   360→    "render_followup_nudges",
   361→    "render_queue_header",
   362→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   363→    "render_terminal_items",
   364→    "scorecard_subjective",
   365→    "show_empty_queue",
   366→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   367→]
```

```
1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     7→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     8→    QueueBuildOptions,
     9→    build_work_queue,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→
    14→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    15→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
    16→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    17→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    18→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    19→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
    20→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
    21→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    22→        if key not in lookup:
    23→            lookup[key] = detectors
    24→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
    25→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    27→        normalized_display = display.lower()
    28→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
    29→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    30→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
    31→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
    32→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
    33→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
    34→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
    35→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
    36→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
    37→    return lookup
    38→
    39→
    40→def try_dimension_rewrite(pattern: str) -> str | None:
    41→    """If pattern matches a dimension name/key, return a detector scope. Otherwise None."""
    42→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    43→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    44→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered)
    45→    if not detectors:
    46→        # Try display name form (with spaces)
    47→        detectors = lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    48→    if not detectors:
    49→        return None
    50→    # Return first detector as scope — the work queue will match on detector name.
    51→    # For multi-detector dimensions, we can't pass multiple scopes through the current
    52→    # interface, but most dimensions have one primary detector. Use glob pattern.
    53→    if len(detectors) == 1:
    54→        return detectors[0]
    55→    # For multi-detector dimensions, use wildcard pattern that scope_matches handles
    56→    return None  # Fall through to load_matches_for_dimension
    57→
    58→
    59→def load_matches_for_dimension(
    60→    state: dict,
    61→    pattern: str,
    62→    *,
    63→    status_filter: str,
    64→) -> tuple[list[dict], str | None]:
    65→    """Try to load findings matching a dimension name. Returns (matches, rewritten_pattern)."""
    66→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    67→    lowered = pattern.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    68→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    69→    if not detectors:
    70→        return [], None
    71→
    72→    # Load findings for each detector in this dimension
    73→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
    74→    for detector in detectors:
    75→        matches = load_matches(
    76→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
    77→        )
    78→        all_matches.extend(matches)
    79→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    unique: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in all_matches:
    83→        fid = item.get("id", "")
    84→        if fid not in seen:
    85→            seen.add(fid)
    86→            unique.append(item)
    87→
    88→    # Find the display name for the dimension
    89→    dim_display = pattern
    90→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    91→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    92→            dim_display = dim.name
    93→            break
    94→
    95→    return unique, dim_display
    96→
    97→
    98→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
    99→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   100→    from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
   101→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   102→    if names:
   103→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   104→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   105→
   106→
   107→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   108→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   109→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   110→    pattern = args.pattern
   111→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   112→    if chronic:
   113→        scope = pattern
   114→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   115→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   116→    if not pattern:
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   124→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   125→
   126→
   127→def load_matches(
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    *,
   130→    scope: str | None,
   131→    status_filter: str,
   132→    chronic: bool,
   133→) -> list[dict]:
   134→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   135→    queue = build_work_queue(
   136→        state,
   137→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   138→            count=None,
   139→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   140→            scope=scope,
   141→            status=status_filter,
   142→            include_subjective=False,
   143→            chronic=chronic,
   144→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   145→        ),
   146→    )
   147→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   148→
   149→
   150→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict]):
   151→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches."""
   152→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   153→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   154→    )
   155→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   156→        matches,
   157→        budget=noise_budget,
   158→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   159→    )
   160→    return (
   161→        surfaced_matches,
   162→        hidden_by_detector,
   163→        noise_budget,
   164→        global_noise_budget,
   165→        budget_warning,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [
   170→    "_detector_names_hint",
   171→    "load_matches",
   172→    "load_matches_for_dimension",
   173→    "resolve_noise",
   174→    "resolve_show_scope",
   175→]
   176→
```

```
   267→        "extract shared boilerplate into reusable helpers or base classes",
   268→        needs_judgment=True,
   269→    ),
   270→    "stale_wontfix": DetectorMeta(
   271→        "stale_wontfix",
   272→        "stale wontfix",
   273→        "Code quality",
   274→        "manual_fix",
   275→        "re-evaluate old wontfix decisions — fix, document, or escalate",
   276→    ),
   277→    "concerns": DetectorMeta(
   278→        "concerns",
   279→        "design concerns",
   280→        "Design coherence",
   281→        "refactor",
   282→        "address design concerns confirmed by subjective evaluation",
   283→    ),
   284→    # ── Manual fix ────────────────────────────────────────
   285→    "deprecated": DetectorMeta(
   286→        "deprecated",
   287→        "deprecated",
   288→        "Code quality",
   289→        "manual_fix",
   290→        "remove deprecated symbols or migrate callers",
   291→    ),
   292→    "stale_exclude": DetectorMeta(
   293→        "stale_exclude",
   294→        "stale exclude",
   295→        "Code quality",
   296→        "manual_fix",
   297→        "remove stale exclusion or verify it's still needed",
   298→    ),
   299→    "security": DetectorMeta(
   300→        "security",
   301→        "security",
   302→        "Security",
   303→        "manual_fix",
   304→        "review and fix security findings — prioritize by severity",
   305→    ),
   306→    # ── Subjective review ────────────────────────────────────
   307→    "review": DetectorMeta(
   308→        "review",
   309→        "design review",
   310→        "Test health",
   311→        "refactor",
   312→        "address design quality findings from AI code review",
   313→    ),
   314→    "subjective_review": DetectorMeta(
   315→        "subjective_review",
   316→        "subjective review",
   317→        "Test health",
   318→        "manual_fix",
   319→        "run `desloppify review --prepare` to evaluate files against quality dimensions",
   320→    ),
   321→}
   322→
   323→_DISPLAY_ORDER = list(DISPLAY_ORDER)
   324→
   325→JUDGMENT_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
   326→    name for name, meta in DETECTORS.items() if meta.needs_judgment
   327→)
   328→
   329→_on_register_callbacks: list[Callable[[], None]] = []
   330→
   331→
   332→def on_detector_registered(callback: Callable[[], None]) -> None:
   333→    """Register a callback invoked after register_detector(). No-arg."""
   334→    _on_register_callbacks.append(callback)
   335→
   336→
   337→def register_detector(meta: DetectorMeta) -> None:
   338→    """Register a detector at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   339→    global JUDGMENT_DETECTORS
   340→    DETECTORS[meta.name] = meta
   341→    if meta.name not in _DISPLAY_ORDER:
   342→        _DISPLAY_ORDER.append(meta.name)
   343→    JUDGMENT_DETECTORS = frozenset(
   344→        name for name, m in DETECTORS.items() if m.needs_judgment
   345→    )
   346→    for cb in _on_register_callbacks:
   347→        cb()
   348→
   349→
   350→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   351→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   352→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   353→
   354→
   355→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   356→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   357→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   358→
   359→
   360→_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   361→_ACTION_LABELS = {
   362→    "auto_fix": "fix",
   363→    "reorganize": "move",
   364→    "refactor": "refactor",
   365→    "manual_fix": "manual",
   366→}
   367→
   368→
   369→def dimension_action_type(dim_name: str) -> str:
   370→    """Return a compact action type label for a dimension based on its detectors."""
   371→    best = "manual"
   372→    best_pri = 99
   373→    for d in DETECTORS.values():
   374→        if d.dimension == dim_name:
   375→            pri = _ACTION_PRIORITY.get(d.action_type, 99)
   376→            if pri < best_pri:
   377→                best_pri = pri
   378→                best = d.action_type
   379→    return _ACTION_LABELS.get(best, "manual")
   380→
   381→
   382→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   383→    """Build detector tool metadata keyed by detector name."""
   384→    result = {}
   385→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   386→        entry: dict = {
   387→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   388→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   389→        }
   390→        if d.tool:
   391→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   392→        if d.guidance:
   393→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   394→        result[name] = entry
   395→    return result
   396→
```

```
1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.state import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
```

```
    34→
    35→def show_detector_progress(state: dict):
    36→    """Show per-detector progress bars — the heartbeat of a scan."""
    37→    return presentation_mod.show_detector_progress(
    38→        state,
    39→        state_mod=state_mod,
    40→        narrative_mod=narrative_mod,
    41→        registry_mod=registry_mod,
    42→        colorize_fn=colorize,
    43→    )
    44→
    45→
    46→def _dimension_bar(score: float, *, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    47→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    48→    return presentation_mod.dimension_bar(score, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    49→
    50→
    51→def scorecard_dimension_entries(
    52→    state: dict,
    53→    *,
    54→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> list[dict]:
    56→    """Return scorecard rows with presentation-friendly metadata."""
    57→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    58→    subjective_by_name = {
    59→        entry["name"]: entry
    60→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(
    61→            state,
    62→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    63→        )
    64→    }
    65→    entries: list[dict] = []
    66→    for name, data in rows:
    67→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    68→        subjective_meta = subjective_by_name.get(name)
    69→        is_subjective = subjective_meta is not None
    70→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    71→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    72→        issues = int(data.get("issues", 0))
    73→        checks = int(data.get("checks", 0))
    74→        placeholder = bool(subjective_meta.get("placeholder")) if subjective_meta else False
    75→        not_scanned = bool(
    76→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    77→        )
    78→        carried_forward = bool(
    79→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    80→        )
    81→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    82→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    83→        cli_keys = (
    84→            list(subjective_meta.get("cli_keys", []))
    85→            if subjective_meta
    86→            else scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data)
    87→        )
    88→        entries.append(
    89→            {
    90→                "name": name,
    91→                "score": score,
    92→                "strict": strict,
    93→                "issues": issues,
    94→                "checks": checks,
    95→                "subjective": is_subjective,
    96→                "placeholder": placeholder,
    97→                "stale": stale,
    98→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
    99→                "not_scanned": not_scanned,
   100→                "carried_forward": carried_forward,
   101→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   102→            }
   103→        )
   104→    return entries
   105→
   106→
   107→def show_scorecard_subjective_measures(state: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Show canonical scorecard dimensions only (mechanical + subjective)."""
   109→    entries = scorecard_dimension_entries(state)
   110→    if not entries:
   111→        return
   112→
   113→    print(colorize("  Scorecard dimensions (matches scorecard.png):", "dim"))
   114→    for entry in entries:
   115→        if entry.get("not_scanned"):
   116→            print(
   117→                "  "
   118→                + f"{entry['name']:<18} "
   119→                + colorize("─── skipped ───────────────────  (run without --skip-slow)", "yellow")
   120→            )
   121→            continue
   122→        bar = _dimension_bar(entry["score"])
   123→        suffix = ""
   124→        if entry.get("carried_forward"):
   125→            suffix = colorize("  ⟲ prior scan", "dim")
   126→        elif entry.get("placeholder"):
   127→            suffix = colorize("  [unassessed]", "yellow")
   128→        elif entry.get("stale"):
   129→            suffix = colorize("  [stale — re-review]", "yellow")
   130→        print(
   131→            "  "
   132→            + f"{entry['name']:<18} {bar} {entry['score']:5.1f}%  "
   133→            + colorize(f"(strict {entry['strict']:5.1f}%)", "dim")
   134→            + suffix
   135→        )
   136→    stale_keys = [e["dimension_key"] for e in entries if e.get("stale")]
   137→    if stale_keys:
   138→        n = len(stale_keys)
   139→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   140→        print(
   141→            colorize(
   142→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   143→                f" — run `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   144→                "yellow",
   145→            )
   146→        )
   147→    print()
   148→
   149→

   152→    return presentation_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(

   155→        colorize_fn=colorize,

   158→
```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    11→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    12→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    18→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    19→
    20→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    21→        return
    22→
    23→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    24→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    25→
    26→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    27→    if output:
    28→        try:
    29→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    30→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    31→            print_agent_plan(["Inspect and execute the generated plan."], next_command=next_command)
    32→        except OSError as e:
    33→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    34→    else:
    35→        print(plan_md)
    36→        print()
    37→        print_agent_plan(["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."], next_command=next_command)
    38→
```

```
    91→
    92→# Detectors where potential = file count but findings are per-(file, sub-type).
    93→# Per-file weighted failures are capped at 1.0 to match the file-based denominator.
    94→FILE_BASED_DETECTORS = {
    95→    detector
    96→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items()
    97→    if policy.file_based
    98→}
    99→
   100→
   101→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   102→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   103→
   104→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   105→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   106→    """
   107→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   108→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   109→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   110→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   111→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   112→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   113→            continue
   114→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   115→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   116→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   117→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   118→    return [
   119→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   120→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   121→    ]
   122→
   123→
   124→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   125→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   126→
   127→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   128→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   129→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   130→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   131→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   132→}
   133→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   134→
   135→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   136→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   137→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   138→
   139→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   140→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   141→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   142→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   143→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   144→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   145→
   146→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   147→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   148→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   149→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   150→
   151→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   152→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   153→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   154→    "file health": 2.0,
   155→    "code quality": 1.0,
   156→    "duplication": 1.0,
   157→    "test health": 1.0,
   158→    "security": 1.0,
   159→}
   160→
   161→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   162→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   163→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   164→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   165→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   166→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   167→    "contracts": 12.0,
   168→    "type safety": 12.0,
   169→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   170→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   171→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   172→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   173→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   174→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   175→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   176→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   177→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   178→}
   179→
   180→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   181→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   182→
   183→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   184→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   185→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   186→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   190→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   191→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   192→

   194→def matches_target_score(
```

```
1→"""Per-detector scoring calculations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     8→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
     9→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    10→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→    detector_policy,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    16→
    17→# Tiered file-count cap thresholds for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    18→# Controls how many findings per file contribute to the weighted failure sum.
    19→_FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD = 6     # findings in file for high cap
    20→_FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD = 3      # findings in file for mid cap
    21→_FILE_CAP_HIGH = 2.0             # cap value at high concentration
    22→_FILE_CAP_MID = 1.5              # cap value at mid concentration
    23→_FILE_CAP_LOW = 1.0              # cap value at low concentration (1-2 findings)
    24→
    25→
    26→def merge_potentials(potentials_by_lang: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    27→    """Sum potentials across languages per detector."""
    28→    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    29→    for lang_potentials in potentials_by_lang.values():
    30→        for detector, count in lang_potentials.items():
    31→            merged[detector] = merged.get(detector, 0) + count
    32→    return merged
    33→
    34→
    35→def _iter_scoring_candidates(
    36→    detector: str,
    37→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    38→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str],
    39→):
    40→    """Yield in-scope findings for a detector (zone-filtered)."""
    41→    for finding in findings.values():
    42→        if finding.get("detector") != detector:
    43→            continue
    44→        if finding.get("zone", "production") in excluded_zones:
    45→            continue
    46→        yield finding
    47→
    48→
    49→def _finding_weight(finding: Finding, *, use_loc_weight: bool) -> float:
    50→    """Compute the scoring weight for a single finding."""
    51→    if use_loc_weight:
    52→        return finding.get("detail", {}).get("loc_weight", 1.0)
    53→    return CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(finding.get("confidence", "medium"), 0.7)
    54→
    55→
    56→def _file_count_cap(findings_in_file: int) -> float:
    57→    """Tiered cap for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    58→
    59→    Keeps file-count denominator semantics while preserving concentration signal:
    60→    1-2 findings => _FILE_CAP_LOW, 3-5 => _FILE_CAP_MID, 6+ => _FILE_CAP_HIGH.
    61→    """
    62→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD:
    63→        return _FILE_CAP_HIGH
    64→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD:
    65→        return _FILE_CAP_MID
    66→    return _FILE_CAP_LOW
    67→
    68→
    69→@dataclass
    70→class _ModeAccum:
    71→    """Per-mode accumulator for file-based detector scoring."""
    72→
    73→    by_file: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    74→    by_file_count: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    75→    file_cap: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    76→    holistic_sum: float = 0.0
    77→    issue_count: int = 0
    78→
    79→
    80→def _file_based_failures_by_mode(
    81→    detector: str,
    82→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    83→    policy,
    84→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]]:
    85→    """Accumulate weighted failures by score mode for file-based detectors."""
    86→    accum: dict[ScoreMode, _ModeAccum] = {mode: _ModeAccum() for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    87→
    88→    for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones):
    89→        status = finding.get("status", "open")
    90→        holistic = finding.get("file") == "." and finding.get("detail", {}).get(
    91→            "holistic"
    92→        )
    93→
    94→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    95→            if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
    96→                continue
    97→
    98→            if holistic:
    99→                accum[mode].holistic_sum += (
   100→                    _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False) * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   101→                )
   102→                accum[mode].issue_count += 1
   103→                continue
   104→
   105→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=policy.use_loc_weight)
   106→            file_key = finding.get("file", "")
   107→            a = accum[mode]
   108→            a.by_file[file_key] = a.by_file.get(file_key, 0.0) + weight
   109→            a.by_file_count[file_key] = a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0) + 1
   110→            if policy.use_loc_weight and file_key not in a.file_cap:
   111→                a.file_cap[file_key] = weight
   112→            a.issue_count += 1
   113→
   114→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]] = {}
   115→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   116→        a = accum[mode]
   117→        if policy.use_loc_weight:
   118→            weighted = sum(
   119→                min(weighted_sum, a.file_cap.get(file_key, weighted_sum))
   120→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   121→            )
   122→        else:
   123→            weighted = sum(
   124→                min(weighted_sum, _file_count_cap(a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0)))
   125→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   126→            )
   127→        out[mode] = (a.issue_count, weighted + a.holistic_sum)
   128→    return out
   129→
   130→
   131→def detector_stats_by_mode(
   132→    detector: str,
   133→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   134→    potential: int,
   135→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]]:
   136→    """Compute (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures) for each score mode."""
   137→    if potential <= 0:
   138→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   139→
   140→    # Review and concern findings are scored via subjective assessments only —
   141→    # exclude them from the detection-side scoring pipeline so resolving these
   142→    # findings never changes the score directly.
   143→    if detector in ("review", "concerns"):
   144→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   145→
   146→    policy = detector_policy(detector)
   147→
   148→    if policy.file_based:
   149→        mode_failures = _file_based_failures_by_mode(detector, findings, policy)
   150→    else:
   151→        issue_count: dict[ScoreMode, int] = {mode: 0 for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   152→        weighted_failures: dict[ScoreMode, float] = {
   153→            mode: 0.0 for mode in SCORING_MODES
   154→        }
   155→
   156→        for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(
   157→            detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones
   158→        ):
   159→            status = finding.get("status", "open")
   160→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False)
   161→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   162→                if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
   163→                    continue
   164→                issue_count[mode] += 1
   165→                weighted_failures[mode] += weight
   166→
   167→        mode_failures = {
   168→            mode: (issue_count[mode], weighted_failures[mode]) for mode in SCORING_MODES
   169→        }
   170→
   171→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]] = {}
   172→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   173→        issues, weighted = mode_failures[mode]
   174→        pass_rate = max(0.0, (potential - weighted) / potential)
   175→        out[mode] = (pass_rate, issues, weighted)
   176→    return out
   177→
   178→
   179→def detector_pass_rate(
   180→    detector: str,
   181→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   182→    potential: int,
   183→    *,
   184→    strict: bool = False,
   185→) -> tuple[float, int, float]:
   186→    """Pass rate for one detector.
   187→
   188→    Returns (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures).
   189→    Zero potential -> (1.0, 0, 0.0).
   190→    """
   191→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   192→    return detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)[mode]
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "detector_pass_rate",
   197→    "detector_stats_by_mode",
   198→    "merge_potentials",
   199→]
   200→
```

```
1→"""Scan merge/update operations for persisted findings state."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "MergeScanOptions",
    10→    "merge_scan",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_findings import (
    14→    auto_resolve_disappeared,
    15→    find_suspect_detectors,
    16→    upsert_findings,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (
    19→    _append_scan_history,
    20→    _build_merge_diff,
    21→    _compute_suppression,
    22→    _merge_scan_inputs,
    23→    _record_scan_metadata,
    24→)

    27→    StateModel,

    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    if new_count > 0 or auto_resolved > 0 or reopened_count > 0:
   175→        changed_detectors = set(current_by_detector.keys())
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
```

```
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (

    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"

    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
```

```
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
```
